 293→        data['fluorescence'] = np.concatenate(fluor_list, axis=0).astype(np.float64)\n   294→        data['neuropil'] = np.concatenate(neuropil_list, axis=0).astype(np.float64)\n   295→        data['n_cells'] = data['fluorescence'].shape[0]\n   296→\n   297→        # Frame rate\n   298→        rate = fluor_grp[plane_keys[0]]['starting_time'].attrs['rate']\n   299→        data['rate'] = float(rate)\n   300→        data['is_dual_plane'] = len(plane_keys) > 1\n   301→\n   302→        # Behavioral data\n   303→        data['position'] = bts['position']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n   304→        data['speed'] = bts['speed']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n   305→        data['lick'] = bts['lick']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n   306→        data['environment'] = bts['environment']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n   307→        data['reward_zone'] = bts['reward_zone']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n   308→        data['trial_number'] = bts['trial number']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n   309→        data['trial_start'] = bts['trial_start']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n   310→        data['teleport'] = bts['teleport']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n   311→        data['behavior_timestamps'] = bts['position']['timestamps'][:]\n   312→\n   313→        # Truncate to minimum length across neural and behavioral data\n   314→        n_neural = data['fluorescence'].shape[1]\n   315→        n_behav = len(data['position'])\n   316→        n_min = min(n_neural, n_behav)\n   317→        if n_neural != n_behav:\n   318→            data['fluorescence'] = data['fluorescence'][:, :n_min]\n   319→            data['neuropil'] = data['neuropil'][:, :n_min]\n   320→            data['position'] = data['position'][:n_min]\n   321→            data['speed'] = data['speed'][:n_min]\n   322→            data['lick'] = data['lick'][:n_min]\n   323→            data['environment'] = data['environment'][:n_min]\n   324→            data['reward_zone'] = data['reward_zone'][:n_min]\n   325→            data['trial_number'] = data['trial_number'][:n_min]\n   326→            data['trial_start'] = data['trial_start'][:n_min]\n   327→            data['teleport'] = data['teleport'][:n_min]\n   328→            data['behavior_timestamps'] = data['behavior_timestamps'][:n_min]\n   329→\n   330→        # Reward events\n   331→        reward_ts_data = bts['Reward']\n   332→        data['reward_timestamps'] = reward_ts_data['timestamps'][:]\n   333→\n   334→        # Subject info\n   335→        data['subject_id'] = f['general']['subject']['subject_id'][()].decode()\n   336→        data['session_id'] = f['general']['session_id'][()].decode()\n   337→\n   338→    return data\n   339→\n   340→\n   341→def get_trial_boundaries(trial_start_signal, teleport_signal, trial_number_signal):\n   342→    \"\"\"Extract trial start and end frame indices.\"\"\"\n   343→    starts = np.where(trial_start_signal == 1)[0]\n   344→    teleports = np.where(teleport_signal == 1)[0]\n   345→\n   346→    trial_starts = []\n   347→    trial_ends = []\n   348→\n   349→    for s in starts:\n   350→        # Find next teleport after this start\n   351→        next_teleports = teleports[teleports > s]\n   352→        if len(next_teleports) > 0:\n   353→            trial_starts.append(s)\n   354→            trial_ends.append(next_teleports[0])  # teleport frame (exclusive end)\n   355→\n   356→    return trial_starts, trial_ends\n   357→\n   358→\n   359→def process_session(filepath, show_processing=False, session_label=\"\"):\n   360→    \"\"\"Process one NWB file into decoder format.\"\"\"\n   361→    t0 = time.time()\n   362→\n   363→    # Load data\n   364→    raw = load_nwb_session(filepath)\n   365→    t_load = time.time() - t0\n   366→    print(f\"  [{session_label}] Loaded: {raw['n_cells']} cells, rate={raw['rate']:.2f} Hz, \"\n   367→          f\"dual_plane={raw['is_dual_plane']}, time={t_load:.1f}s\")\n   368→\n   369→    # Get trial boundaries\n   370→    trial_starts, trial_ends = get_trial_boundaries(\n   371→        raw['trial_start'], raw['teleport'], raw['trial_number'])\n   372→    n_trials = len(trial_starts)\n   373→    print(f\"  [{session_label}] Trials: {n_trials}\")\n   374→\n   375→    if n_trials < 2:\n   376→        print(f\"  [{session_label}] SKIP: fewer than 2 trials\")\n   377→        return None\n   378→\n   379→    # Compute dF/F\n   380→    t1 = time.time()\n   381→    dff = compute_dff(raw['fluorescence'], raw['neuropil'], trial_starts, trial_ends)\n   382→    t_dff = time.time() - t1\n   383→    print(f\"  [{session_label}] dF/F computed: time={t_dff:.1f}s\")\n   384→\n   385→    # Detect and remove interneurons\n   386→    speed_for_corr = raw['speed'].copy()\n   387→    nanmask = ~np.isnan(dff[0, :])\n   388→    is_interneuron = detect_interneurons(dff, speed_for_corr, nanmask)\n   389→    n_interneurons = is_interneuron.sum()\n   390→    print(f\"  [{session_label}] Interneurons: {n_interneurons}/{raw['n_cells']} \"\n   391→          f\"({100*n_interneurons/max(1,raw['n_cells']):.2f}%)\")\n   392→\n   393→    # Filter neurons\n   394→    keep_mask = ~is_interneuron\n   395→    dff = dff[keep_mask]\n   396→    n_cells = dff.shape[0]\n   397→\n   398→    if n_cells == 0:\n   399→        print(f\"  [{session_label}] SKIP: no cells after filtering\")\n   400→        return None\n   401→\n   402→    # Detect lick errors\n   403→    lick_error_trials = detect_lick_errors(raw['lick'], trial_starts, trial_ends)\n   404→    print(f\"  [{session_label}] Lick error trials: {len(lick_error_trials)}\")\n   405→\n   406→    # Determine reward zone per trial\n   407→    zone_labels = determine_reward_zone(raw['position'], raw['reward_zone'],\n   408→                                         trial_starts, trial_ends)\n   409→\n   410→    # Determine reward per trial\n   411→    rewarded = determine_reward_per_trial(\n   412→        raw['reward_timestamps'], raw['behavior_timestamps'],\n   413→        raw['trial_number'], trial_starts, trial_ends)\n   414→\n   415→    # Determine environment per trial\n   416→    environments = []\n   417→    for start, end in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n   418→        env_vals = raw['environment'][start:end]\n   419→        active_env = env_vals[env_vals >= 0]\n   420→        if len(active_env) > 0:\n   421→            environments.append(int(round(active_env[0])))\n   422→        else:\n   423→            environments.append(0)\n   424→\n   425→    # Downsample dual-plane data\n   426→    downsample = 2 if raw['is_dual_plane'] else 1\n   427→    effective_rate = raw['rate'] / downsample\n   428→\n   429→    # Build trial arrays\n   430→    neural_trials = []\n   431→    input_trials = []\n   432→    output_trials = []\n   433→    valid_trial_indices = []\n   434→\n   435→    for i in range(n_trials):\n   436→        if i in lick_error_trials:\n   437→            continue\n   438→\n   439→        start = trial_starts[i]\n   440→        end = trial_ends[i]\n   441→\n   442→        # Extract trial data\n   443→        trial_neural = dff[:, start:end]\n   444→        trial_pos = raw['position'][start:end]\n   445→        trial_speed = raw['speed'][start:end]\n   446→        trial_lick = raw['lick'][start:end]\n   447→\n   448→        # Skip trials with all-NaN neural data\n   449→        if np.all(np.isnan(trial_neural)):\n   450→            continue\n   451→\n   452→        # Downsample if dual-plane\n   453→        if downsample > 1:\n   454→            T = trial_neural.shape[1]\n   455→            T_new = T // downsample\n   456→            if T_new < 2:\n   457→                continue\n   458→            trial_neural = trial_neural[:, :T_new * downsample].reshape(n_cells, T_new, downsample).mean(axis=2)\n   459→            trial_pos = trial_pos[:T_new * downsample].reshape(T_new, downsample).mean(axis=1)\n   460→            trial_speed = trial_speed[:T_new * downsample].reshape(T_new, downsample).mean(axis=1)\n   461→            trial_lick = trial_lick[:T_new * downsample].reshape(T_new, downsample).max(axis=1)\n   462→\n   463→        T = trial_neural.shape[1]\n   464→        if T < 2:\n   465→            continue\n   466→\n   467→        # Replace NaN in neural with 0\n   468→        trial_neural = np.nan_to_num(trial_neural, nan=0.0).astype(np.float32)\n   469→\n   470→        # --- INPUTS (4, T) ---\n   471→        time_from_start = np.arange(T, dtype=np.float32) / effective_rate\n   472→        env = np.full(T, environments[i], dtype=np.float32)\n   473→        trial_num = np.full(T, i, dtype=np.float32)\n   474→        prev_outcome = np.full(T, rewarded[i - 1] if i > 0 else 0, dtype=np.float32)\n   475→        inputs = np.stack([time_from_start, env, trial_num, prev_outcome], axis=0)\n   476→\n   477→        # --- OUTPUTS (6, T) ---\n   478→        # Reward zone for this trial\n   479→        zone_label = zone_labels[i]\n   480→        zone_idx = ZONE_LABELS.index(zone_label) if zone_label in ZONE_LABELS else 0\n   481→        zone_start, zone_end = REWARD_ZONES[zone_label] if zone_label else (80, 130)\n   482→\n   483→        # Distance to reward zone\n   484→        distance = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(trial_pos, zone_start, zone_end)\n   485→        dist_bins = discretize_distance(distance)\n   486→\n   487→        # Position bins\n   488→        pos_bins = discretize_position(trial_pos)\n   489→\n   490→        # Speed bins\n   491→        speed_bins = discretize_speed(np.abs(trial_speed))\n   492→\n   493→        # Lick binary\n   494→        lick_binary = (trial_lick > 0).astype(int)\n   495→\n   496→        # Per-trial outputs broadcast\n   497→        rz_loc = np.full(T, zone_idx, dtype=int)\n   498→        reward_out = np.full(T, rewarded[i], dtype=int)\n   499→\n   500→        outputs = np.stack([dist_bins, pos_bins, speed_bins, lick_binary, rz_loc, reward_out], axis=0).astype(int)\n   501→\n   502→        neural_trials.append(trial_neural)\n   503→        input_trials.append(inputs)\n   504→        output_trials.append(outputs)\n   505→        valid_trial_indices.append(i)\n   506→\n   507→    if len(neural_trials) < 2:\n   508→        print(f\"  [{session_label}] SKIP: fewer than 2 valid trials\")\n   509→        return None\n   510→\n   511→    t_total = time.time() - t0\n   512→    print(f\"  [{session_label}] Done: {len(neural_trials)} valid trials, \"\n   513→          f\"{n_cells} cells, time={t_total:.1f}s\")\n   514→\n   515→    # Show processing plots\n   516→    if show_processing and len(neural_trials) > 0:\n   517→        plot_processing(raw, dff, trial_starts, trial_ends, zone_labels, rewarded,\n   518→                       neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials, valid_trial_indices,\n   519→                       session_label, effective_rate)\n   520→\n   521→    result = {\n   522→        'neural': neural_trials,\n   523→        'input': input_trials,\n   524→        'output': output_trials,\n   525→        'n_cells': n_cells,\n   526→        'n_interneurons': n_interneurons,\n   527→        'n_lick_errors': len(lick_error_trials),\n   528→        'subject_id': raw['subject_id'],\n   529→        'session_id': raw['session_id'],\n   530→        'rate': effective_rate,\n   531→    }\n   532→    return result\n   533→\n   534→\n   535→def plot_processing(raw, dff, trial_starts, trial_ends, zone_labels, rewarded,\n   536→                   neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials, valid_trial_indices,\n   537→                   session_label, effective_rate):\n   538→    \"\"\"Plot processing steps for visual verification.\"\"\"\n   539→    fig, axes = plt.subplots(5, 2, figsize=(20, 20))\n   540→    fig.suptitle(f'Processing: {session_label}', fontsize=14)\n   541→\n   542→    # Pick 2 example trials\n   543→    example_indices = [0, min(len(neural_trials) - 1, len(neural_trials) // 2)]\n   544→\n   545→    for col, eidx in enumerate(example_indices):\n   546→        trial_idx = valid_trial_indices[eidx]\n   547→        start, end = trial_starts[trial_idx], trial_ends[trial_idx]\n   548→\n   549→        # Row 0: Raw fluorescence (first 5 cells)\n   550→        ax = axes[0, col]\n   551→        n_show = min(5, raw['fluorescence'].shape[0])\n   552→        for c in range(n_show):\n   553→            ax.plot(raw['fluorescence'][c, start:end], alpha=0.5, label=f'Cell {c}')\n   554→        ax.set_title(f'Trial {trial_idx}: Raw Fluorescence')\n   555→        ax.set_ylabel('F')\n   556→\n   557→        # Row 1: dF/F (first 5 cells)\n   558→        ax = axes[1, col]\n   559→        for c in range(min(5, dff.shape[0])):\n   560→            ax.plot(dff[c, start:end], alpha=0.5)\n   561→        ax.set_title(f'dF/F (after processing)')\n   562→        ax.set_ylabel('dF/F')\n   563→\n   564→        # Row 2: Position and reward zone\n   565→        ax = axes[2, col]\n   566→        pos = raw['position'][start:end]\n   567→        ax.plot(pos, 'b-', label='Position')\n   568→        zl = zone_labels[trial_idx]\n   569→        if zl and zl in REWARD_ZONES:\n   570→            zs, ze = REWARD_ZONES[zl]\n   571→            ax.axhspan(zs, ze, alpha=0.2, color='green', label=f'Zone {zl}')\n   572→        ax.set_title(f'Position & RZ (zone={zl}, rew={rewarded[trial_idx]})')\n   573→        ax.set_ylabel('Position (cm)')\n   574→        ax.legend(fontsize=8)\n   575→\n   576→        # Row 3: Decoder outputs\n   577→        ax = axes[3, col]\n   578→        T = output_trials[eidx].shape[1]\n   579→        t = np.arange(T) / effective_rate\n   580→        ax.plot(t, output_trials[eidx][0], label='dist_rz_bin')\n   581→        ax.plot(t, output_trials[eidx][1], label='pos_bin')\n   582→        ax.plot(t, output_trials[eidx][2], label='speed_bin')\n   583→        ax.plot(t, output_trials[eidx][3], label='lick')\n   584→        ax.set_title('Decoder Outputs')\n   585→        ax.legend(fontsize=8)\n   586→\n   587→        # Row 4: Speed and lick\n   588→        ax = axes[4, col]\n   589→        ax.plot(raw['speed'][start:end], 'g-', label='Speed')\n   590→        ax2 = ax.twinx()\n   591→        ax2.plot(raw['lick'][start:end], 'r-', alpha=0.5, label='Lick')\n   592→        ax.set_title('Speed & Lick (raw)')\n   593→        ax.set_xlabel('Frame')\n   594→        ax.set_ylabel('Speed (cm/s)')\n   595→        ax2.set_ylabel('Lick count')\n   596→\n   597→    fig.tight_layout()\n   598→    safe_label = session_label.replace('/', '_').replace(' ', '_')\n   599→    fig.savefig(f'/app/processing_{safe_label}.png', dpi=100)\n   600→    plt.close(fig)\n   601→    print(f\"  Saved processing plot: processing_{safe_label}.png\")\n   602→\n   603→\n   604→def collect_nwb_files(data_dir, sample=False):\n   605→    \"\"\"Collect all NWB files, sorted by subject then session.\"\"\"\n   606→    files = []\n   607→    for subj_dir in sorted(os.listdir(data_dir)):\n   608→        if not subj_dir.startswith('sub-'):\n   609→            continue\n   610→        subj_path = os.path.join(data_dir, subj_dir)\n   611→        for nwb_file in sorted(os.listdir(subj_path)):\n   612→            if nwb_file.endswith('.nwb'):\n   613→                files.append(os.path.join(subj_path, nwb_file))\n   614→\n   615→    if sample:\n   616→        # Return 2 sessions from different subjects (pick small ones)\n   617→        # Pick m11 ses-03 (small) and m19 ses-01 (medium)\n   618→        sample_files = []\n   619→        for f in files:\n   620→            if 'sub-m11_ses-03' in f or 'sub-m19_ses-01' in f:\n   621→                sample_files.append(f)\n   622→        return sample_files if sample_files else files[:2]\n   623→\n   624→    return files\n   625→\n   626→\n   627→def main():\n   628→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Convert NWB data to decoder format.')\n   629→    parser.add_argument('outfile', type=str, help='Output pickle file path')\n   630→    parser.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True, help='Process all sessions (default)')\n   631→    parser.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='Process only 2 sessions for testing')\n   632→    parser.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true', help='Plot processing visualizations')\n   633→    args = parser.parse_args()\n   634→\n   635→    if args.sample:\n   636→        args.full = False\n   637→\n   638→    nwb_files = collect_nwb_files(DATA_DIR, sample=args.sample)\n   639→    print(f\"Found {len(nwb_files)} NWB files to process\")\n   640→\n   641→    # Process all sessions\n   642→    all_results = []\n   643→    total_t0 = time.time()\n   644→\n   645→    for i, filepath in enumerate(nwb_files):\n   646→        basename = os.path.basename(filepath)\n   647→        label = basename.replace('_behavior+ophys.nwb', '')\n   648→        print(f\"\\n[{i+1}/{len(nwb_files)}] Processing {label}\")\n   649→\n   650→        try:\n   651→            result = process_session(filepath, show_processing=args.show_processing,\n   652→                                    session_label=label)\n   653→            if result is not None:\n   654→                all_results.append(result)\n   655→        except Exception as e:\n   656→            print(f\"  ERROR processing {label}: {e}\")\n   657→            import traceback\n   658→            traceback.print_exc()\n   659→\n   660→    total_time = time.time() - total_t0\n   661→    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n   662→    print(f\"Processed {len(all_results)} sessions in {total_time:.1f}s\")\n   663→\n   664→    if len(all_results) == 0:\n   665→        print(\"ERROR: No valid sessions!\")\n   666→        sys.exit(1)\n   667→\n   668→    # Build final data structure\n   669→    data = build_data_dict(all_results)\n   670→\n   671→    # Print summary\n   672→    print_summary(data)\n   673→\n   674→    # Save\n   675→    with open(args.outfile, 'wb') as f:\n   676→        pickle.dump(data, f)\n   677→    print(f\"\\nSaved to {args.outfile} ({os.path.getsize(args.outfile) / 1e6:.1f} MB)\")\n   678→\n   679→\n   680→def build_data_dict(results):\n   681→    \"\"\"Build the final data dictionary from processed sessions.\"\"\"\n   682→    # Collect unique subjects\n   683→    subjects = sorted(set(r['subject_id'] for r in results))\n   684→    subject_to_idx = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n   685→\n   686→    neural = []\n   687→    inputs = []\n   688→    outputs = []\n   689→    subject_idx = []\n   690→    brain_region_idx_list = []\n   691→\n   692→    for r in results:\n   693→        neural.append(r['neural'])\n   694→        inputs.append(r['input'])\n   695→        outputs.append(r['output'])\n   696→        subject_idx.append(subject_to_idx[r['subject_id']])\n   697→        # All neurons are from CA1\n   698→        brain_region_idx_list.append(np.zeros(r['n_cells'], dtype=int))\n   699→\n   700→    data = {\n   701→        'neural': neural,\n   702→        'input': inputs,\n   703→        'output': outputs,\n   704→        'subjects': subjects,\n   705→        'subject_idx': np.array(subject_idx, dtype=int),\n   706→        'brain_regions': ['CA1'],\n   707→        'brain_region_idx': brain_region_idx_list,\n   708→        'input_names': [\n   709→            'time_from_trial_start',\n   710→            'environment',\n   711→            'trial_number',\n   712→            'previous_trial_outcome',\n   713→        ],\n   714→        'output_names': [\n   715→            'distance_to_reward_zone',\n   716→            'absolute_position',\n   717→            'speed',\n   718→            'lick',\n   719→            'reward_zone_location',\n   720→            'reward_outcome',\n   721→        ],\n   722→        'output_values': [\n   723→            ['< -50cm', '-50 to -10cm', '-10 to 0cm', '0cm (in zone)', '>0 to +10cm', '+10 to +50cm', '> +50cm'],\n   724→            ['0-90cm', '90-180cm', '180-270cm', '270-360cm', '360-450cm'],\n   725→            ['< 2cm/s', '2-10cm/s', '10-20cm/s', '20-40cm/s', '> 40cm/s'],\n   726→            ['no lick', 'lick'],\n   727→            ['Zone A', 'Zone B', 'Zone C'],\n   728→            ['no reward', 'reward'],\n   729→        ],\n   730→        'metadata': {\n   731→            'task_description': ('Hidden reward zone navigation task on 450cm virtual linear track. '\n   732→                                'Mice navigate to find hidden 50cm reward zone (A/B/C) across two '\n   733→                                'environments with reward zone switches every few days.'),\n   734→            'time_bin_size': 1000.0 / TARGET_RATE,  # cm\nREWARD_ZONES = {\n    'A': (80, 130),\n    'B': (200, 250),\n    'C': (320, 370),\n}\nZONE_LABELS = ['A', 'B', 'C']\nNEUROPIL_COEF = 0.7\nBASELINE_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 15    # frames\nBASELINE_MINMAX_WINDOW = 300  # frames (~20 s at 15.5 Hz)\nDFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 2          # frames\nINTERNEURON_R_THRESH = 0.5    # Pearson r threshold (paper)\nLICK_ERROR_THRESH = 0.3       # fraction of frames with lick>2 (paper: >30%)\nTARGET_RATE = 15.5078125       # Hz, canonical frame rate\n\n\n# ─── Helper functions ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef nansmooth(a, sigma, axis=-1):\n    \"\"\"Gaussian smoothing that handles NaNs (matching reference code).\"\"\"\n    nan_inds = np.isnan(a)\n    a_clean = np.copy(a)\n    a_clean[nan_inds] = 0\n    weights = np.ones(a.shape)\n    weights[nan_inds] = 0.001\n    a_smooth = ndi.gaussian_filter1d(a_clean, sigma, axis=axis)\n    w_smooth = ndi.gaussian_filter1d(weights, sigma, axis=axis)\n    return a_smooth / w_smooth\n\n\ndef compute_dff(f_raw, f_neu, trial_starts, trial_ends):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute dF/F following reference preprocessing.py:dff().\n\n    Args:\n        f_raw: (n_cells, n_timepoints) raw fluorescence\n        f_neu: (n_cells, n_timepoints) neuropil fluorescence\n        trial_starts: list of trial start frame indices (0-based)\n        trial_ends: list of trial end frame indices (0-based, exclusive)\n\n    Returns:\n        dff: (n_cells, n_timepoints) with NaN outside trials\n    \"\"\"\n    n_cells, n_time = f_raw.shape\n\n    # Initialize with NaN\n    f_ = np.full_like(f_raw, np.nan)\n    f_neu_ = np.full_like(f_neu, np.nan)\n\n    # Keep only trial data (exclude ITI)\n    for start, end in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n        f_[:, start:end] = f_raw[:, start:end]\n        f_neu_[:, start:end] = f_neu[:, start:end]\n\n    # Neuropil subtraction\n    f_ = f_ - NEUROPIL_COEF * f_neu_\n\n    # Baseline computation per trial\n    baseline = np.full_like(f_, np.nan)\n    dff = np.full_like(f_, np.nan)\n\n    for start, end in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n        trial_data = f_[:, start:end]\n\n        # Add back neuropil mean per trial (reference: lines 433-435)\n        neuropil_mean = np.nanmean(f_neu_[:, start:end], axis=1, keepdims=True)\n        trial_data = trial_data + NEUROPIL_COEF * neuropil_mean\n        f_[:, start:end] = trial_data\n\n        # Maximin baseline\n        # Step 1: Smooth with sigma=15\n        smoothed = nansmooth(trial_data, BASELINE_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n        # Step 2: Minimum filter (300 frames = ~20s)\n        bl = ndi.minimum_filter1d(smoothed, BASELINE_MINMAX_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n        # Step 3: Maximum filter (dilation)\n        bl = ndi.maximum_filter1d(bl, BASELINE_MINMAX_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n        baseline[:, start:end] = bl\n\n    # Compute dF/F\n    nanmask = ~np.isnan(f_[0, :])\n    dff[:, nanmask] = (f_[:, nanmask] - baseline[:, nanmask]) / np.abs(baseline[:, nanmask])\n\n    # Smooth dF/F per trial\n    for start, end in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n        dff[:, start:end] = nansmooth(dff[:, start:end], DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n\n    return dff\n\n\ndef detect_interneurons(dff, speed, nanmask=None):\n    \"\"\"Detect putative interneurons by speed-dFF correlation (paper: r > 0.5).\"\"\"\n    n_cells = dff.shape[0]\n    if nanmask is None:\n        nanmask = ~np.isnan(dff[0, :])\n\n    is_interneuron = np.zeros(n_cells, dtype=bool)\n    for c in range(n_cells):\n        valid = nanmask & ~np.isnan(dff[c, :]) & ~np.isnan(speed)\n        if valid.sum() < 10:\n            continue\n        r = np.corrcoef(dff[c, valid], speed[valid])[0, 1]\n        if r > INTERNEURON_R_THRESH:\n            is_interneuron[c] = True\n    return is_interneuron\n\n\ndef detect_lick_errors(lick, trial_starts, trial_ends, threshold=LICK_ERROR_THRESH):\n    \"\"\"Detect trials with stuck lick sensor (paper: >30% frames with lick>2).\"\"\"\n    error_trials = []\n    for i, (start, end) in enumerate(zip(trial_starts, trial_ends)):\n        trial_licks = lick[start:end]\n        n_frames = len(trial_licks)\n        if n_frames == 0:\n            continue\n        frac_high = np.sum(trial_licks > 2) / n_frames\n        if frac_high > threshold:\n            error_trials.append(i)\n    return error_trials\n\n\ndef determine_reward_zone(position, reward_zone_signal, trial_starts, trial_ends):\n    \"\"\"\n    Determine reward zone label (A, B, or C) for each trial.\n    Uses mean position where reward_zone > 0.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(trial_starts)\n    zone_labels = [None] * n_trials\n\n    for i, (start, end) in enumerate(zip(trial_starts, trial_ends)):\n        rz = reward_zone_signal[start:end]\n        pos = position[start:end]\n        mask = rz > 0\n        if mask.sum() > 0:\n            mean_pos = pos[mask].mean()\n            # Assign to closest zone center\n            best_zone = None\n            best_dist = float('inf')\n            for label, (zs, ze) in REWARD_ZONES.items():\n                center = (zs + ze) / 2\n                dist = abs(mean_pos - center)\n                if dist < best_dist:\n                    best_dist = dist\n                    best_zone = label\n            zone_labels[i] = best_zone\n\n    # Fill missing labels by inheriting from nearest neighbor\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        if zone_labels[i] is None:\n            # Search forward and backward\n            for d in range(1, n_trials):\n                if i - d >= 0 and zone_labels[i - d] is not None:\n                    zone_labels[i] = zone_labels[i - d]\n                    break\n                if i + d < n_trials and zone_labels[i + d] is not None:\n                    zone_labels[i] = zone_labels[i + d]\n                    break\n\n    return zone_labels\n\n\ndef determine_reward_per_trial(reward_timestamps, behavior_timestamps, trial_number_signal,\n                                trial_starts, trial_ends):\n    \"\"\"Determine which trials received reward from reward event timestamps.\"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(trial_starts)\n    rewarded = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=int)\n\n    if len(reward_timestamps) == 0:\n        return rewarded\n\n    # Match each reward timestamp to closest behavior frame\n    for rt in reward_timestamps:\n        frame_idx = np.argmin(np.abs(behavior_timestamps - rt))\n        trial_num = int(trial_number_signal[frame_idx])\n        if trial_num >= 0 and trial_num < n_trials:\n            rewarded[trial_num] = 1\n\n    return rewarded\n\n\ndef compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position, zone_start, zone_end):\n    \"\"\"Compute signed distance from position to nearest point in reward zone.\"\"\"\n    distance = np.zeros_like(position)\n    before = position < zone_start\n    inside = (position >= zone_start) & (position <= zone_end)\n    after = position > zone_end\n    distance[before] = position[before] - zone_start\n    distance[inside] = 0\n    distance[after] = position[after] - zone_end\n    return distance\n\n\ndef discretize_distance(distance):\n    \"\"\"Discretize distance to reward zone into 7 bins.\"\"\"\n    bins = np.zeros(len(distance), dtype=int)\n    bins[distance < -50] = 0\n    bins[(distance >= -50) & (distance < -10)] = 1\n    bins[(distance >= -10) & (distance < 0)] = 2\n    bins[distance == 0] = 3  # inside reward zone or exactly at boundary\n    bins[(distance > 0) & (distance <= 10)] = 4\n    bins[(distance > 10) & (distance <= 50)] = 5\n    bins[distance > 50] = 6\n    return bins\n\n\ndef discretize_position(position):\n    \"\"\"Discretize position into 5 equal bins (90 cm each).\"\"\"\n    bins = np.zeros(len(position), dtype=int)\n    bins[position < 90] = 0\n    bins[(position >= 90) & (position < 180)] = 1\n    bins[(position >= 180) & (position < 270)] = 2\n    bins[(position >= 270) & (position < 360)] = 3\n    bins[position >= 360] = 4\n    return bins\n\n\ndef discretize_speed(speed):\n    \"\"\"Discretize speed into 5 bins.\"\"\"\n    bins = np.zeros(len(speed), dtype=int)\n    bins[speed < 2] = 0\n    bins[(speed >= 2) & (speed < 10)] = 1\n    bins[(speed >= 10) & (speed < 20)] = 2\n    bins[(speed >= 20) & (speed < 40)] = 3\n    bins[speed >= 40] = 4\n    return bins\n\n\ndef load_nwb_session(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load all needed data from an NWB file.\"\"\"\n    data = {}\n    with h5py.File(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        ophys = f['processing']['ophys']\n        bts = f['processing']['behavior']['BehavioralTimeSeries']\n\n        # Image segmentation\n        seg = ophys['ImageSegmentation']['PlaneSegmentation']\n        iscell = seg['iscell'][:]\n        plane_idx = seg['planeIdx'][:]\n\n        # Determine planes\n        fluor_grp = ophys['Fluorescence']\n        plane_keys = sorted(fluor_grp.keys())\n\n        # Load fluorescence and neuropil for all planes\n        fluor_list = []\n        neuropil_list = []\n        cell_mask_list = []\n\n        offset = 0\n        for pk in plane_keys:\n            n_rois = fluor_grp[pk]['data'].shape[1]\n            plane_num = int(pk.replace('plane', ''))\n\n            # Get iscell mask for this plane\n            plane_mask = plane_idx == plane_num\n            plane_iscell = iscell[plane_mask, 0].astype(bool)\n\n            # Load fluorescence (timepoints x ROIs) -> transpose to (ROIs x timepoints)\n            fl = fluor_grp[pk]['data'][:].T  # (n_rois, T)\n\n            # Load neuropil\n            neu_key = pk  # neuropil keys match plane keys\n            neu = ophys['Neuropil'][pk]['data'][:].T  # (n_rois, T)\n\n            fluor_list.append(fl[plane_iscell])\n            neuropil_list.append(neu[plane_iscell])\n            cell_mask_list.append(plane_iscell)\n            offset += n_rois\n\n        data['fluorescence'] = np.concatenate(fluor_list, axis=0).astype(np.float64)\n        data['neuropil'] = np.concatenate(neuropil_list, axis=0).astype(np.float64)\n        data['n_cells'] = data['fluorescence'].shape[0]\n\n        # Frame rate\n        rate = fluor_grp[plane_keys[0]]['starting_time'].attrs['rate']\n        data['rate'] = float(rate)\n        data['is_dual_plane'] = len(plane_keys) > 1\n\n        # Behavioral data\n        data['position'] = bts['position']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n        data['speed'] = bts['speed']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n        data['lick'] = bts['lick']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n        data['environment'] = bts['environment']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n        data['reward_zone'] = bts['reward_zone']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n        data['trial_number'] = bts['trial number']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n        data['trial_start'] = bts['trial_start']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n        data['teleport'] = bts['teleport']['data'][:].astype(np.float64)\n        data['behavior_timestamps'] = bts['position']['timestamps'][:]\n\n        # Truncate to minimum length across neural and behavioral data\n        n_neural = data['fluorescence'].shape[1]\n        n_behav = len(data['position'])\n        n_min = min(n_neural, n_behav)\n        if n_neural != n_behav:\n            data['fluorescence'] = data['fluorescence'][:, :n_min]\n            data['neuropil'] = data['neuropil'][:, :n_min]\n            data['position'] = data['position'][:n_min]\n            data['speed'] = data['speed'][:n_min]\n            data['lick'] = data['lick'][:n_min]\n            data['environment'] = data['environment'][:n_min]\n            data['reward_zone'] = data['reward_zone'][:n_min]\n            data['trial_number'] = data['trial_number'][:n_min]\n            data['trial_start'] = data['trial_start'][:n_min]\n            data['teleport'] = data['teleport'][:n_min]\n            data['behavior_timestamps'] = data['behavior_timestamps'][:n_min]\n\n        # Reward events\n        reward_ts_data = bts['Reward']\n        data['reward_timestamps'] = reward_ts_data['timestamps'][:]\n\n        # Subject info\n        data['subject_id'] = f['general']['subject']['subject_id'][()].decode()\n        data['session_id'] = f['general']['session_id'][()].decode()\n\n    return data\n\n\ndef get_trial_boundaries(trial_start_signal, teleport_signal, trial_number_signal):\n    \"\"\"Extract trial start and end frame indices.\"\"\"\n    starts = np.where(trial_start_signal == 1)[0]\n    teleports = np.where(teleport_signal == 1)[0]\n\n    trial_starts = []\n    trial_ends = []\n\n    for s in starts:\n        # Find next teleport after this start\n        next_teleports = teleports[teleports > s]\n        if len(next_teleports) > 0:\n            trial_starts.append(s)\n            trial_ends.append(next_teleports[0])  # teleport frame (exclusive end)\n\n    return trial_starts, trial_ends\n\n\ndef process_session(filepath, show_processing=False, session_label=\"\"):\n    \"\"\"Process one NWB file into decoder format.\"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n\n    # Load data\n    raw = load_nwb_session(filepath)\n    t_load = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"  [{session_label}] Loaded: {raw['n_cells']} cells, rate={raw['rate']:.2f} Hz, \"\n          f\"dual_plane={raw['is_dual_plane']}, time={t_load:.1f}s\")\n\n    # Get trial boundaries\n    trial_starts, trial_ends = get_trial_boundaries(\n        raw['trial_start'], raw['teleport'], raw['trial_number'])\n    n_trials = len(trial_starts)\n    print(f\"  [{session_label}] Trials: {n_trials}\")\n\n    if n_trials < 2:\n        print(f\"  [{session_label}] SKIP: fewer than 2 trials\")\n        return None\n\n    # Compute dF/F\n    t1 = time.time()\n    dff = compute_dff(raw['fluorescence'], raw['neuropil'], trial_starts, trial_ends)\n    t_dff = time.time() - t1\n    print(f\"  [{session_label}] dF/F computed: time={t_dff:.1f}s\")\n\n    # Detect and remove interneurons\n    speed_for_corr = raw['speed'].copy()\n    nanmask = ~np.isnan(dff[0, :])\n    is_interneuron = detect_interneurons(dff, speed_for_corr, nanmask)\n    n_interneurons = is_interneuron.sum()\n    print(f\"  [{session_label}] Interneurons: {n_interneurons}/{raw['n_cells']} \"\n          f\"({100*n_interneurons/max(1,raw['n_cells']):.2f}%)\")\n\n    # Filter neurons\n    keep_mask = ~is_interneuron\n    dff = dff[keep_mask]\n    n_cells = dff.shape[0]\n\n    if n_cells == 0:\n        print(f\"  [{session_label}] SKIP: no cells after filtering\")\n        return None\n\n    # Detect lick errors\n    lick_error_trials = detect_lick_errors(raw['lick'], trial_starts, trial_ends)\n    print(f\"  [{session_label}] Lick error trials: {len(lick_error_trials)}\")\n\n    # Determine reward zone per trial\n    zone_labels = determine_reward_zone(raw['position'], raw['reward_zone'],\n                                         trial_starts, trial_ends)\n\n    # Determine reward per trial\n    rewarded = determine_reward_per_trial(\n        raw['reward_timestamps'], raw['behavior_timestamps'],\n        raw['trial_number'], trial_starts, trial_ends)\n\n    # Determine environment per trial\n    environments = []\n    for start, end in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n        env_vals = raw['environment'][start:end]\n        active_env = env_vals[env_vals >= 0]\n        if len(active_env) > 0:\n            environments.append(int(round(active_env[0])))\n        else:\n            environments.append(0)\n\n    # Downsample dual-plane data\n    downsample = 2 if raw['is_dual_plane'] else 1\n    effective_rate = raw['rate'] / downsample\n\n    # Build trial arrays\n    neural_trials = []\n    input_trials = []\n    output_trials = []\n    valid_trial_indices = []\n\n    for i in range(n_trials):\n        if i in lick_error_trials:\n            continue\n\n        start = trial_starts[i]\n        end = trial_ends[i]\n\n        # Extract trial data\n        trial_neural = dff[:, start:end]\n        trial_pos = raw['position'][start:end]\n        trial_speed = raw['speed'][start:end]\n        trial_lick = raw['lick'][start:end]\n\n        # Skip trials with all-NaN neural data\n        if np.all(np.isnan(trial_neural)):\n            continue\n\n        # Downsample if dual-plane\n        if downsample > 1:\n            T = trial_neural.shape[1]\n            T_new = T // downsample\n            if T_new < 2:\n                continue\n            trial_neural = trial_neural[:, :T_new * downsample].reshape(n_cells, T_new, downsample).mean(axis=2)\n            trial_pos = trial_pos[:T_new * downsample].reshape(T_new, downsample).mean(axis=1)\n            trial_speed = trial_speed[:T_new * downsample].reshape(T_new, downsample).mean(axis=1)\n            trial_lick = trial_lick[:T_new * downsample].reshape(T_new, downsample).max(axis=1)\n\n        T = trial_neural.shape[1]\n        if T < 2:\n            continue\n\n        # Replace NaN in neural with 0\n        trial_neural = np.nan_to_num(trial_neural, nan=0.0).astype(np.float32)\n\n        # --- INPUTS (4, T) ---\n        time_from_start = np.arange(T, dtype=np.float32) / effective_rate\n        env = np.full(T, environments[i], dtype=np.float32)\n        trial_num = np.full(T, i, dtype=np.float32)\n        prev_outcome = np.full(T, rewarded[i - 1] if i > 0 else 0, dtype=np.float32)\n        inputs = np.stack([time_from_start, env, trial_num, prev_outcome], axis=0)\n\n        # --- OUTPUTS (6, T) ---\n        # Reward zone for this trial\n        zone_label = zone_labels[i]\n        zone_idx = ZONE_LABELS.index(zone_label) if zone_label in ZONE_LABELS else 0\n        zone_start, zone_end = REWARD_ZONES[zone_label] if zone_label else (80, 130)\n\n        # Distance to reward zone\n        distance = compute_distance_to_reward_zone(trial_pos, zone_start, zone_end)\n        dist_bins = discretize_distance(distance)\n\n        # Position bins\n        pos_bins = discretize_position(trial_pos)\n\n        # Speed bins\n        speed_bins = discretize_speed(np.abs(trial_speed))\n\n        # Lick binary\n        lick_binary = (trial_lick > 0).astype(int)\n\n        # Per-trial outputs broadcast\n        rz_loc = np.full(T, zone_idx, dtype=int)\n        reward_out = np.full(T, rewarded[i], dtype=int)\n\n        outputs = np.stack([dist_bins, pos_bins, speed_bins, lick_binary, rz_loc, reward_out], axis=0).astype(int)\n\n        neural_trials.append(trial_neural)\n        input_trials.append(inputs)\n        output_trials.append(outputs)\n        valid_trial_indices.append(i)\n\n    if len(neural_trials) < 2:\n        print(f\"  [{session_label}] SKIP: fewer than 2 valid trials\")\n        return None\n\n    t_total = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"  [{session_label}] Done: {len(neural_trials)} valid trials, \"\n          f\"{n_cells} cells, time={t_total:.1f}s\")\n\n    # Show processing plots\n    if show_processing and len(neural_trials) > 0:\n        plot_processing(raw, dff, trial_starts, trial_ends, zone_labels, rewarded,\n                       neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials, valid_trial_indices,\n                       session_label, effective_rate)\n\n    result = {\n        'neural': neural_trials,\n        'input': input_trials,\n        'output': output_trials,\n        'n_cells': n_cells,\n        'n_interneurons': n_interneurons,\n        'n_lick_errors': len(lick_error_trials),\n        'subject_id': raw['subject_id'],\n        'session_id': raw['session_id'],\n        'rate': effective_rate,\n    }\n    return result\n\n\ndef plot_processing(raw, dff, trial_starts, trial_ends, zone_labels, rewarded,\n                   neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials, valid_trial_indices,\n                   session_label, effective_rate):\n    \"\"\"Plot processing steps for visual verification.\"\"\"\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(5, 2, figsize=(20, 20))\n    fig.suptitle(f'Processing: {session_label}', fontsize=14)\n\n    # Row 2: Position and reward zone\n        ax = axes[2, col]\n        pos = raw['position'][start:end]\n        ax.plot(pos, 'b-', label='Position')\n        zl = zone_labels[trial_idx]\n        if zl and zl in REWARD_ZONES:\n            zs, ze = REWARD_ZONES[zl]\n            ax.axhspan(zs, ze, alpha=0.2, color='green', label=f'Zone {zl}')\n        ax.set_title(f'Position & RZ (zone={zl}, rew={rewarded[trial_idx]})')\n        ax.set_ylabel('Position (cm)')\n        ax.legend(fontsize=8)\n\n        # Row 3: Decoder outputs\n        ax = axes[3, col]\n        T = output_trials[eidx].shape[1]\n        t = np.arange(T) / effective_rate\n        ax.plot(t, output_trials[eidx][0], label='dist_rz_bin')\n        ax.plot(t, output_trials[eidx][1], label='pos_bin')\n        ax.plot(t, output_trials[eidx][2], label='speed_bin')\n        ax.plot(t, output_trials[eidx][3], label='lick')\n        ax.set_title('Decoder Outputs')\n        ax.legend(fontsize=8)\n\n        # Row 4: Speed and lick\n        ax = axes[4, col]\n        ax.plot(raw['speed'][start:end], 'g-', label='Speed')\n        ax2 = ax.twinx()\n        ax2.plot(raw['lick'][start:end], 'r-', alpha=0.5, label='Lick')\n        ax.set_title('Speed & Lick (raw)')\n        ax.set_xlabel('Frame')\n        ax.set_ylabel('Speed (cm/s)')\n        ax2.set_ylabel('Lick count')\n\n    fig.tight_layout()\n    safe_label = session_label.replace('/', '_').replace(' ', '_')\n    fig.savefig(f'/app/processing_{safe_label}.png', dpi=100)\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print(f\"  Saved processing plot: processing_{safe_label}.png\")\n\n\ndef collect_nwb_files(data_dir, sample=False):\n    \"\"\"Collect all NWB files, sorted by subject then session.\"\"\"\n    files = []\n    for subj_dir in sorted(os.listdir(data_dir)):\n        if not subj_dir.startswith('sub-'):\n            continue\n        subj_path = os.path.join(data_dir, subj_dir)\n        for nwb_file in sorted(os.listdir(subj_path)):\n            if nwb_file.endswith('.nwb'):\n                files.append(os.path.join(subj_path, nwb_file))\n\n    if sample:\n        # More informative than deconvolved events for neural network decoder. Reference method: neuropil subtract (0.7), add back neuropil mean per trial, maximin baseline (smooth sigma=15, min filter 300, max filter 300), dF/F = (F-baseline)/|baseline|, smooth sigma=2.\n2. **Cell filtering: iscell + interneuron exclusion (r>0.5)**: Use Suite2P manual curation (iscell[:,0]==1) then exclude putative interneurons with speed-dFF correlation > 0.5 (paper threshold). find nans\n   125→    nan_inds = np.isnan(a)\n   126→    a_nanless = np.copy(a)\n   127→    # make nans 0\n   128→    a_nanless[nan_inds] = 0\n   129→\n   130→    # inversely weight nanned indices\n   131→    one = np.ones(a.shape)\n   132→    one[nan_inds] = 0.001\n   133→    a_nanless = ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(a_nanless, sig, axis=axis, **kwargs) # 1:stop -\n   238→                 1] = nansmooth(f_[:, start-1:stop-1], [0, 15])\n   239→\n   240→            # minimum filter, taking min val over 20 sec\n   241→            flow[:, start-1:stop-1] = ndimage.minimum_filter1d(\n   242→                flow[:, start-1:stop-1], int(300), axis=-1) # was sp.ndimage.filters.minimum_filter1d\n   243→            flow[:, start-1:stop-1] = ndimage.maximum_filter1d(\n   244→                flow[:, start-1:stop-1], int(300), axis=-1)  # max filter with same window (dilation), was sp.ndimage.filters.maximum_filter1d\n   245→\n   246→    # to get deltaF/F: subtract baseline from initial signal, divide by abs(baseline)\n   247→    # baseline can some times end up as negative due to regression\n   248→    dff = np.zeros(f_.shape) * np.nan\n   249→    if subtract_baseline:\n   250→        dff[:, nanmask] = (f_[:, nanmask] - flow[:, nanmask]\n   251→                           ) / np.abs(flow[:, nanmask])\n   252→\n   253→    print('dff:', dff.shape, 'min ', np.nanmin(dff), 'max ', np.nanmax(dff))\n   254→\n   255→    # Smooth the deltaF/F transients by 2 time bins\n   256→    for i, (start, stop) in enumerate(zip(start_inds, stop_inds)):\n   257→        dff[:, start - 1:stop -\n   258→            1] = nansmooth_1d(dff[:, start - 1:stop - 1], 2, axis=1)\n   259→        # After smoothing: Get \"spikes\" with calcium kernel deconvolution from suite2p\n   260→        if deconvolve:\n   261→            spks[:, start-1:stop-1] = dcnv.oasis(\n   262→                np.ascontiguousarray(dff[:, start-1:stop-1], dtype=np.float32),\n   263→                2000, tau, (frame_rate/n_planes)) # this is float64 in the original code\n   264→\n   265→    if deconvolve:\n   266→        return dff, spks\n   267→    else:\n   268→        return dff\n   269→\n   270→\n   271→def is_putative_interneuron(dff, speed, r_thresh=0.5):\n   272→    \"\"\"\n   273→    Find putative interneurons based on correlation of dF/F with speed\n   274→    (under the hypothesis that fast-spiking interneurons in the pyramidal\n   275→    layer are often highly correlated with running speed).\n   276→\n   277→    :param dff: (ncells, T) dF/F timeseries, NaN outside the analysed windows\n   278→    :param speed: (T,) running speed in cm/s\n   279→    :param r_thresh: threshold for speed correlation r; dayData sets int_thresh = 0.5, which is\n   280→        the value the Methods quote (\"a Pearson correlation of >0.5\"). Note that the threshold\n   281→        defaulted to 0.3 in the source function; the analyses always pass 0.5.\n   282→    :return: is_int (array) with a Boolean for each ROI, where putative interneuron is True.\n   283→\n   284→    ** copied from src/reward_relative/spatial.py::is_putative_interneuron **\n   285→    Change: takes the dF/F and speed arrays as arguments instead of reading them off a TwoPUtils \n   286→    `sess` object, which this conversion does not build.\n   287→    \"\"\"\n   288→    speed_corr = np.zeros((dff.shape[0],))\n   289→    nanmask = ~np.isnan(dff[0, :])\n   290→\n   291→    for c in range(dff.shape[0]):\n   292→        speed_corr[c] = np.corrcoef(\n   293→            dff[c, nanmask], speed[nanmask])[0, 1]\n   294→\n   295→    is_int = speed_corr > r_thresh\n   296→\n   297→    return is_int\n   298→\n   299→# end copy-paste\n   300→# =================================================================================================\n   301→\n   302→\n   303→def get_subjects(datadir=DATADIR):\n   304→    # find all directories in datadir that start with 'sub-'\n   305→    subjects = [d.split('-')[1] for d in os.listdir(datadir) if d.startswith('sub-')]\n   306→    return subjects\n   307→\n   308→def get_sessions(subject, datadir=DATADIR):\n   309→    subject_dir = os.path.join(datadir, f\"sub-{subject}\")\n   310→    # parse sessions from files named sub-<subject>-<session>_behavior+ophys.nwb\n   311→    nwb_files = [f for f in os.listdir(subject_dir) if f.endswith('.nwb')]\n   312→    sessions = [int(f.split('_')[1].split('-')[1]) for f in nwb_files]\n   313→    return sessions\n   314→\n   315→def get_nwb_file(subject, session, datadir=DATADIR):\n   316→    nwb_file_path = os.path.join(datadir, f\"sub-{subject}\", f\"sub-{subject}_ses-{session:02d}_behavior+ophys.nwb\")\n   317→    assert os.path.exists(nwb_file_path), f\"File {nwb_file_path} does not exist\"\n   318→    return nwb_file_path\n   319→\n   320→def interp(new_idx,old_idx,old_data,**kwargs):\n   321→    f = interp1d(old_idx, old_data, axis=0, **kwargs)\n   322→    return f(new_idx)\n   323→\n   324→\n   325→def get_trial_starts_ends(trial_start,teleport):\n   326→    trial_start_idx = np.nonzero(trial_start)[0]\n   327→    is_teleport_start = (teleport[1:] > 0) & (teleport[:-1] <= 0)\n   328→    trial_end_idx = np.nonzero(is_teleport_start)[0] + 1 \n   329→    assert len(trial_start_idx) == len(trial_end_idx), f\"Number of trial starts ({len(trial_start_idx)}) does not match number of trial ends ({len(trial_end_idx)})\"\n   330→    return trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx\n   331→        \n   332→\n   333→def survey_session_data(subject, session, datadir=DATADIR):\n   334→    nwb_file_path = get_nwb_file(subject, session, datadir)\n   335→    info = {}\n   336→    info['reward_zone_positions'] = []\n   337→    with pynwb.NWBHDF5IO(nwb_file_path, 'r') as io:\n   338→        nwbfile = io.read()\n   339→        # parse neural activity data\n   340→        deconvolved_data = nwbfile.processing[\"ophys\"][\"Deconvolved\"]\n   341→        spike_data = []\n   342→        nplanes = len(deconvolved_data.roi_response_series)\n   343→        for plane_data in deconvolved_data.roi_response_series.values():\n   344→            roi_data = plane_data.rois[:]\n   345→            iscell = np.array(roi_data['iscell'].to_list())[:,0] == 1\n   346→            spike_data_curr = plane_data.data[:]\n   347→            assert spike_data_curr.shape[1] == iscell.shape[0], f\"Number of neurons in data ({spike_data_curr.shape[1]}) does not match number of iscell entries ({iscell.shape[0]})\"\n   348→            spike_data.append(spike_data_curr[:,iscell])\n   349→        spike_data = np.concatenate(spike_data, axis=1)\n   350→        info['T'], info['nneurons'] = spike_data.shape\n   351→        max_spike_rate = np.max(spike_data)\n   352→        info['max_spike_rate_range'] = (max_spike_rate.min(), max_spike_rate.max())\n   353→        info['neural_time_bin_size'] = nplanes/plane_data.rate*1000 # ms\n   354→\n   355→        behavior_data = nwbfile.processing['behavior']['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series       \n   356→        istrial = behavior_data['trial number'].data[:] >= 0\n   357→        # reward_amounts are all the same\n   358→        reward_amounts = behavior_data['Reward'].data[:]\n   359→        info['unique_reward_amounts'] = np.unique(reward_amounts)\n   360→        info['nrewards'] = len(reward_amounts)\n   361→        # autoreward is all 0s, so not useful for modeling, but check that it is all zeros\n   362→        autoreward = behavior_data['autoreward'].data[:]\n   363→        info['unique_autoreward'] = np.unique(autoreward[istrial])\n   364→        # vr environment\n   365→        vr_environment = behavior_data['environment'].data[:] # one value per time point, does this change by session?\n   366→        info['unique_vr_environment'] = np.unique(vr_environment[istrial])\n   367→        licks = behavior_data['lick'].data[:]\n   368→        info['unique_licks'] = np.unique(licks[istrial])\n   369→        position = behavior_data['position'].data[:]\n   370→        info['position_range'] = (position[istrial].min(), position[istrial].max())\n   371→        reward_zone = behavior_data['reward_zone'].data[:]\n   372→        info['unique_reward_zone'] = np.unique(reward_zone[istrial])\n   373→        scanning = behavior_data['scanning'].data[:]\n   374→        info['unique_scanning'] = np.unique(scanning[istrial])\n   375→        speed = behavior_data['speed'].data[:]\n   376→        info['speed_range'] = (speed[istrial].min(), speed[istrial].max())\n   377→        teleport = behavior_data['teleport'].data[:]\n   378→        info['unique_teleport'] = np.unique(teleport[istrial])\n   379→        \n   380→        # trial number and trial start do not agree with each other\n   381→        # use trial_start and teleport to find starts and ends of trials\n   382→        trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx = get_trial_starts_ends(behavior_data['trial_start'].data[:], teleport)\n   383→        trial_length = trial_end_idx - trial_start_idx\n   384→        info['trial_length_range'] = (trial_length.min(), trial_length.max())\n   385→\n   386→        ntrials = len(trial_start_idx)\n   387→        info['ntrials'] = ntrials\n   388→        info['reward_zone_positions'] = np.full((ntrials, 2), np.nan)\n   389→        for trial in range(ntrials):\n   390→            idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial], trial_end_idx[trial], dtype=int)\n   391→            pos_trial = position[idx]\n   392→            reward_zone_trial = reward_zone[idx] > 0\n   393→            if not np.any(reward_zone_trial):\n   394→                continue\n   395→            info['reward_zone_positions'][trial] = [np.min(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial]), np.max(pos_trial[reward_zone_trial])]\n   396→                    \n   397→    return info\n   398→    \n   399→def get_bin_centers(bin_edges,data):\n   400→    # replace infinity with the max/min of the data for plotting purposes\n   401→    bin_edges_plot = np.array(bin_edges.copy())\n   402→    if np.isinf(bin_edges_plot[0]):\n   403→        bin_edges_plot[0] = data.min()\n   404→        if bin_edges_plot[0] > bin_edges_plot[1]:\n   405→            bin_edges_plot[0] = 2*bin_edges_plot[1] - bin_edges_plot[2] # extrapolate to get a lower value for the first bin edge\n   406→    if np.isinf(bin_edges_plot[-1]):\n   407→        bin_edges_plot[-1] = data.max()\n   408→        if bin_edges_plot[-1] < bin_edges_plot[-2]:\n   409→            bin_edges_plot[-1] = 2*bin_edges_plot[-2] - bin_edges_plot[-3] # extrapolate to get a higher value for the last bin edge\n   410→    bin_centers = (bin_edges_plot[:-1] + bin_edges_plot[1:]) / 2\n   411→    return bin_centers\n   412→    \n   413→def compute_distance_to_reward_zone(position_curr, reward_zone_curr):\n   414→    ntimepoints = len(position_curr)\n   415→    reward_zone_range = reward_zone_dict[reward_zone_curr]\n   416→    distance_to_reward_zone = np.zeros(ntimepoints)\n   417→    idx_before_zone = position_curr < reward_zone_range[0]\n   418→    distance_to_reward_zone[idx_before_zone] = position_curr[idx_before_zone] - reward_zone_range[0] # negative if before the zone\n   419→    idx_after_zone = position_curr > reward_zone_range[1]\n   420→    distance_to_reward_zone[idx_after_zone] = position_curr[idx_after_zone] - reward_zone_range[1] # positive if after the zone\n   421→    return distance_to_reward_zone\n   422→    \n   423→def convert_session_data(subject, session, info, trial_offset, time_bin_size=None, datadir=DATADIR, \n   424→                         show_processing=False, max_neurons_plot=30, min_ntimepoints=50,\n   425→                         figfile=None):\n   426→\n   427→    nwb_file_path = get_nwb_file(subject, session, datadir)\n   428→    io = pynwb.NWBHDF5IO(nwb_file_path, 'r')\n   429→    nwbfile = io.read()\n   430→\n   431→    # parse neural activity data: the raw suite2p traces the paper computes its dF/F and\n   432→    # deconvolved events from. The deconvolution needs the trial boundaries, so it runs further\n   433→    # below, once those have been parsed out of the behavior data.\n   434→    fluorescence_data = nwbfile.processing[\"ophys\"][\"Fluorescence\"]\n   435→    neuropil_data = nwbfile.processing[\"ophys\"][\"Neuropil\"]\n   436→    fluorescence = []\n   437→    neuropil = []\n   438→    nplanes = len(fluorescence_data.roi_response_series)\n   439→    for plane_name, plane_data in fluorescence_data.roi_response_series.items():\n   440→        roi_data = plane_data.rois[:]\n   441→        iscell = np.array(roi_data['iscell'].to_list())[:,0] == 1\n   442→        fluorescence_curr = plane_data.data[:]\n   443→        neuropil_curr = neuropil_data.roi_response_series[plane_name].data[:]\n   444→        assert fluorescence_curr.shape[1] == iscell.shape[0], f\"Number of neurons in data ({fluorescence_curr.shape[1]}) does not match number of iscell entries ({iscell.shape[0]})\"\n   445→        assert neuropil_curr.shape == fluorescence_curr.shape, f\"Neuropil shape ({neuropil_curr.shape}) does not match fluorescence shape ({fluorescence_curr.shape})\"\n   446→        # Subset to the curated cells now rather than after dff. Every step of dff is per-cell --\n   447→        # its only cross-cell operation is a Gaussian with sigma 0 on the cell axis -- so this is\n   448→        # numerically identical to subsetting afterwards, on a third of the memory.\n   449→        fluorescence.append(fluorescence_curr[:,iscell])\n   450→        neuropil.append(neuropil_curr[:,iscell])\n   451→    T = fluorescence[0].shape[0]\n   452→    # The rate stored on the series is the scanner rate: with two planes interleaved it is twice\n   453→    # the rate at which any one plane is sampled, which is why dff takes n_planes as well.\n   454→    frame_rate = plane_data.rate\n   455→    neural_time_bin_size = nplanes/frame_rate*1000 # ms\n   456→\n   457→    behavior_data = nwbfile.processing['behavior']['BehavioralTimeSeries'].time_series\n   458→    \n   459→    timestamps = behavior_data['trial number'].timestamps[:] \n   460→    if T != len(timestamps):\n   461→        print(f\"Warning: number of neural time bins ({T}) does not match number of behavior timestamps ({len(timestamps)}), cropping to the minimum of the two\")\n   462→        T = min(T, len(timestamps))\n   463→        fluorescence = [f[:T] for f in fluorescence]\n   464→        neuropil = [f[:T] for f in neuropil]\n   465→        timestamps = timestamps[:T]\n   466→    \n   467→    behavior_time_bin_size = np.median(np.diff(timestamps))*1000 # ms\n   468→    \n   469→    # trial number for each time point\n   470→    trial_start_idx, trial_end_idx = get_trial_starts_ends(behavior_data['trial_start'].data[:], behavior_data['teleport'].data[:])\n   471→    ntrials = len(trial_start_idx)\n   472→    \n   473→    # reward_amounts are all the same\n   474→    reward_amounts = behavior_data['Reward'].data[:][:T]\n   475→    assert np.allclose(reward_amounts,reward_amounts[0]), f\"Reward amounts are not all the same, unique values: {np.unique(reward_amounts)}\"\n   476→    reward_times = behavior_data['Reward'].timestamps[:] # in seconds        \n   477→    # find the closest timestamp to reward times, not careful for mismatches right now\n   478→    reward_time_indices = np.searchsorted(timestamps, reward_times)\n   479→    err = timestamps[reward_time_indices] - reward_times\n   480→    assert np.all(np.abs(err) <= time_bin_size/2), f\"Reward times do not align with neural time bins, max error: {np.max(np.abs(err))} s\"\n   481→    isreward = np.zeros(T)\n   482→    isreward[reward_time_indices] = 1\n   483→\n   484→    # autoreward is all 0s, so not useful for modeling, but check that it is all zeros\n   485→\n   486→    # vr environment\n   487→    vr_environment = behavior_data['environment'].data[:][:T] # one value per time point, does this change by session?\n   488→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['environment'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for environment do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   489→\n   490→    # lick\n   491→    licks = behavior_data['lick'].data[:][:T]\n   492→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['lick'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for lick do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   493→\n   494→    # current position in the VR environment\n   495→    position = behavior_data['position'].data[:][:T]\n   496→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['position'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for position do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   497→\n   498→    # teleport -- not sure what to do with this, maybe should end the trial early??\n   499→    #isteleport = behavior_data['teleport'].data[:][:T] == 1\n   500→    #assert np.allclose(behavior_data['teleport'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for teleport do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   501→\n   502→    # speed\n   508→    speed = behavior_data['speed'].data[:][:T] # cm / s\n   509→    assert np.allclose(behavior_data['speed'].timestamps[:][:T], timestamps), \"Timestamps for speed do not match trial number timestamps\"\n   510→\n   511→    io.close()\n   512→\n   513→    assert len(timestamps) == T, f\"Number of timestamps ({len(timestamps)}) does not match number of neural time bins ({T})\"\n   514→    if time_bin_size is not None:\n   515→        assert np.isclose(neural_time_bin_size, time_bin_size, rtol=1e-2), f\"Neural time bin size ({neural_time_bin_size} ms) does not match specified time bin size ({time_bin_size} ms)\"\n   516→        assert np.isclose(behavior_time_bin_size, time_bin_size, rtol=1e-2), f\"Behavior time bin size ({behavior_time_bin_size} ms) does not match specified time bin size ({time_bin_size} ms)\"\n   517→    \n   518→    # neural activity: the paper's deconvolved events, computed per plane because the\n   519→    # deconvolution kernel depends on the per-plane sampling rate, frame_rate/nplanes.\n   520→    #\n   521→    # The released subject id m<N> is the paper's internal animal GCAMP<N>: methods.txt describes\n   522→    # exactly the two groups this table holds -- \"except for mice m11-m14 on task days 1, 7, 8 and\n   523→    # Session\n   525→    # number is the experiment day: m11 starts at ses-03, and the paper says imaging for m11\n   526→    # started on day 3.\n   527→    keep_teleports = session in teleport_sessions.get(f'GCAMP{subject[1:]}', {}).get('exp_days', [])\n   528→    spike_data = []\n   529→    nint = 0\n   530→    for fluorescence_curr, neuropil_curr in zip(fluorescence, neuropil):\n   531→        # The trial indices are passed in shifted by +1: dff slices its windows as\n   532→        # [start-1, stop-1), one sample earlier than the [trial_start, teleport) laps emitted here\n   533→        # -- which is the window their own behavior.py:57 takes from the same two index arrays, so\n   534→        # the two files already disagree by a sample upstream. Unshifted, the last sample of every\n   535→        # lap comes out NaN (80 per session here) and one pre-lap sample gets a value instead;\n   536→        # shifted, dff's windows land exactly on the emitted laps. The one cost is that on\n   537→        # keep_teleports sessions the baseline window opens two samples after the teleport onset\n   538→        # rather than their one.\n   539→        dff_curr, events_curr = dff(fluorescence_curr.T.astype(np.float64),\n   540→                                    pd.Series(trial_start_idx + 1), pd.Series(trial_end_idx + 1),\n   541→                                    f_neu=neuropil_curr.T.astype(np.float64),\n   542→                                    neuropil_method='subtract', baseline_method='maximin',\n   543→                                    subtract_baseline=True, neu_coef=0.7, tau=0.7,\n   544→                                    frame_rate=frame_rate, n_planes=nplanes, deconvolve=True,\n   545→                                    keep_teleports=keep_teleports)\n   546→        # Drop the putative interneurons the paper excludes from further analysis.\n   547→        isint = is_putative_interneuron(dff_curr, speed)\n   548→        nint += int(np.sum(isint))\n   549→        spike_data.append(events_curr[~isint].T)\n   550→    spike_data = np.concatenate(spike_data, axis=1)\n   551→    print(f\"Excluded {nint} putative interneurons of {nint + spike_data.shape[1]} cells \"\n   552→          f\"({100*nint/(nint + spike_data.shape[1]):.2f}%)\")\n   553→    # Samples between laps are NaN by construction (that is what restricting the baseline to the\n   554→    # lap means); samples inside a lap must not be, since those are what the decoder sees.\n   555→    on_trial = np.zeros(T, dtype=bool)\n   556→    for start, undefined somewhere inside a trial\"\n   560→\n   561→    neural = []\n   562→    input = []\n   563→    output = []\n   564→    ninputs = len(input_names)\n   565→    noutputs = len(output_names)\n   566→    istrial = np.ones(ntrials,dtype=bool) # keep track of whether we have dropped a trial for having too few time points\n   567→    min_distance_to_reward_zone = np.inf\n   568→    max_distance_to_reward_zone = -np.inf\n   569→    for trial in range(ntrials):\n   570→        idx = np.arange(trial_start_idx[trial], trial_end_idx[trial], dtype=int)\n   571→        if idx.sum() < istrial[trial] = False\n   574→            continue\n   575→\n   576→        spike_data_curr = spike_data[idx]\n   577→        timestamps_curr = timestamps[idx]\n   578→        vr_environment_curr = min_distance_to_reward_zone)\n   614→        max_distance_to_reward_zone = max(distance_to_reward_zone.max(), max_distance_to_reward_zone)\n   615→\n   616→        # histogram distance to reward zone 771→\n   772→def segment_reward_positions(reward_zone_positions):\n   773→    # assign each trial to one of the reward zones defined in reward_zone_dict\n   774→    # use viterbi algorithm with a high cost for switching to encourage stable assignments\n   775→    zone_names = sorted(reward_zone_dict.keys())\n   776→    n_states = len(zone_names)\n   777→    zone_ranges = np.array([reward_zone_dict[z] for z in zone_names])  # (n_states, 2)\n   778→    pstay = .9\n   779→    sigma = 10.0  # std dev for Gaussian emission on zone distance\n   780→\n   781→    # transition log-probabilities\n   782→    log_trans = np.full((n_states, n_states), np.log((1 - pstay) / (n_states - 1)))\n   783→    np.fill_diagonal(log_trans, np.log(pstay))\n   784→\n   785→    results = {}\n   786→\n   787→    for mouse_id, positions in reward_zone_positions.items():\n   788→        n_trials = positions.shape[0]\n   789→        startpoints = positions[:,0] # use the start point as it looks cleaner\n   790→        valid = ~np.isnan(startpoints)\n   791→\n   792→        # emission log-probabilities: Gaussian penalty based on distance to zone range\n   793→        # distance is 0 if inside [lo, hi], otherwise distance to nearest edge\n   794→        # for NaN trials, uniform emission (all zeros) so assignment is driven by transitions\n   795→        log_emission = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n   796→        dist = np.maximum(zone_ranges[None, :, 0] - startpoints[valid, None],\n   797→                          startpoints[valid, None] - zone_ranges[None, :, 1]) # negative if inside the zone, positive if outside\n   798→        dist = np.maximum(dist, 0)\n   799→        log_emission[valid] = -0.5 * (dist / sigma) ** 2\n   800→\n   801→        # viterbi forward pass\n   802→        log_delta = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states))\n   803→        psi = np.zeros((n_trials, n_states), dtype=int)\n   804→        log_delta[0] = log_emission[0]  # uniform prior\n   805→        for t in range(1, n_trials):\n   806→            for k in range(n_states):\n   807→                scores = log_delta[t - 1] + log_trans[:, k]\n   808→                psi[t, k] = np.argmax(scores)\n   809→                log_delta[t, k] = scores[psi[t, k]] + log_emission[t, k]\n   810→\n   811→        # backtrack\n   812→        states = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=int)\n   813→        states[-1] = np.argmax(log_delta[-1])\n   814→        for t in range(n_trials - 2, -1, -1):\n   815→            states[t] = psi[t + 1, states[t + 1]]\n   816→\n   817→        # convert state indices to zone names\n   818→        zone_labels = np.array([zone_names[s] for s in states])\n   819→\n   820→        # distance from each trial's startpoint to its assigned zone range\n   821→        assigned_lo = zone_ranges[states, 0]\n   822→        assigned_hi = zone_ranges[states, 1]\n   823→        zone_dist = np.maximum(np.maximum(assigned_lo - startpoints, startpoints - assigned_hi), 0)\n   824→        zone_dist[~valid] = np.nan\n   825→\n   826→        results[mouse_id] = {'zone_labels': zone_labels, 'states': states, 'zone_dist': zone_dist}\n   827→\n   828→    return results\n
